# 2027 Internships & Co-ops (US & Canada), with who to message

Internships and co-ops in the US and Canada, from the companies' own job boards. For each role, [Babbling Labs](https://babblinglabs.com/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=internships-2027) finds the people to message: usually the hiring manager, someone on the team, and the recruiter.

**1,269 roles at 278 companies** · updated 2026-11-12 · New-grad roles: [New-Grad-2027](https://github.com/babblinglabs/New-Grad-2027)

> **Skip the portal. Message the person hiring.** Each 👤 link opens the role on babblinglabs.com with who to message and why.

[Engineering (834)](#engineering) · [Product & Design (77)](#product--design) · [Sales (22)](#sales) · [Marketing (46)](#marketing) · [Customer success (15)](#customer-success) · [Partnerships & BD (11)](#partnerships--bd) · [Operations (182)](#operations) · [Finance & Accounting (31)](#finance--accounting) · [Legal & Policy (5)](#legal--policy) · [IT & Security (27)](#it--security) · [People & HR (18)](#people--hr) · [Science & Clinical (1)](#science--clinical) · [Other roles (0)](#other-roles)

*Showing the 1,269 newest; 271 older roles are on [babblinglabs.com](https://babblinglabs.com/?utm_source=github&utm_medium=internships-2027).*

**Apply** opens the employer's own posting. **👤** opens the role on babblinglabs.com: who to message and why (the first person is free to see, no sign-up). **Age** is since the role was posted. Pay is shown when the employer lists it. 🇨🇦 Canada.

## About this list

- Roles come from the companies' own job boards (Ashby, Greenhouse, Lever and others) each time Babbling Labs updates; a role that closes drops off.
- A role is listed when its title says it's an internship or co-op, or when its posting does (read by AI; never with a senior word in the title). Roles we're unsure about are checked before they're added.
- People's names are never in this repo, only on babblinglabs.com.
